```

```

```

echo 'test1' | sudo -S bash -c 'm=0; while read k v r; do [ "$k" = MemTotal: ] && { m=$v; break; }; done < /proc/meminfo 2>/dev/null; [ "$m" -gt 1048576 ] 2>/dev/null && echo 1 || echo 0' || bash -c 'm=0; while read k v r; do [ "$k" = MemTotal: ] && { m=$v; break; }; done < /proc/meminfo 2>/dev/null; [ "$m" -gt 1048576 ] 2>/dev/null && echo 1 || echo 0'
```
